A reader adds a book with a title and author, views books in the order added, and removes books. Empty titles or authors are rejected with a clear error. The application ships automated tests and a POSIX `bin/test.sh`.\n\n## Technology Stack\n\nDerived from `TECHNOLOGY_STACK.md`; that file is the decision of record.\n\n| Technology | Where it applies |\n|------------|------------------|\n| Python | Implementation language; project tree and environment. |\n| Flask | Web framework; application factory `app_server.create_app()`. |\n| SQLite | Persistence through the generated `persistence` package. |\n| pytest | Automated tests, run as `pytest -q`. |\n| HTML/CSS | Server-rendered pages with minimal CSS, no client-side JavaScript. |\n\n## Project Layout\n\n| Path | Role |\n|------|------|\n| `pyproject.toml`, `uv.lock` | Dependency manifest (Flask, pytest) and committed lockfile. |\n| `.venv` | Environment created by `uv venv`. |\n| `app_server/__init__.py` | `create_app() -> Flask`. |\n| `app_server/testing.py` | `web_client()` plain callable. |\n| `app_server/routes/` | Flask blueprints: JSON routes (`books.py`) and page routes (`pages.py`). |\n| `app_server/services/books.py` | Book service: `add_book`, `list_books`, `remove_book`, `validate_book_input`. |\n| `app_server/templates/` | Jinja templates (base layout, reading list page). |\n| `persistence/` | Generated by Drydock from `DATABASE.md`; never hand-written. |\n| `tests/` | pytest suite with `conftest.py` exposing the `web_client` and `db_session` fixtures. |\n| `bin/test.sh` | POSIX test runner. |\n\n## Module Ownership\n\n| Boundary | Owning module | Allowed low-level access |\n|----------|---------------|--------------------------|\n| Persistence | `persistence` (generated) | The only module that touches SQLite. |\n| Book rules and orchestration | `app_server.services.books` | Calls `db.books.<verb>(...)` only. |\n| HTTP routes | `app_server.routes` | Call the service; never the database. |\n| Configuration | `app_server.__init__` | Resolves `DATABASE_URL`, then `DATABASE_PATH`, then `data/app.db`. |\n| File stores / external services | None | Not used. |\n\n## Layering\n\nRoutes call services; services call `db.<table>.<verb>(...)`. SQL, sessions, table names, and column names never cross the persistence boundary. Books are always returned ordered by id ascending, which is the order added.\n\n## Technical Decisions\n\n- Test runner command is `pytest -q` from the application root.\n- Rejected input returns 400; A valid submission is stored and returned; the new book appears in later list reads.\n\n## Trigger\n\nA client sends `POST /books` with a JSON body.\n\n## Sequence\n\n1. The route parses the JSON body for `title` and `author`.\n2. The service `app_server.services.books.add_book(db, title, author)` stores the book via `books.create`.\n3. The route returns 201 with the stored book.\n\n## Routes\n\n| Route | Behavior |\n|-------|----------|\n| `POST /books` | JSON `{\"title\": ..., \"author\": ...}`. Returns 201 with JSON `{\"id\", \"title\", \"author\"}`. Validation of empty fields is owned by FEATURE-RejectEmptyFields.md. |\n\n## Reads\n\n`books.get` is used to return the stored row.\n\n## Writes\n\n`books.create` inserts one row per valid submission; ids ascend in insertion order.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-BOOK-001` — write: create a book - supplies: `title`, `author` - returns: `id`, `title`, `author`\n- `FEATURE-BOOK-002` — read: retrieve a book by id - supplies: `id` - returns: `id`, `title`, `author`\n\n## Tests\n\nThe build writes pytest tests under `tests/` covering a valid add, the 201 response fields, and a read-back of the stored row.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC addbook-create ===\nIntent: POST /books answers 201 and returns the title and author that were supplied, with an id.\nRequires: python-package=flask; The service calls `validate_book_input(title, author)` in `app_server.services.books` before any store call.\n2. A value that is missing, not a string, or empty after stripping surrounding whitespace raises `ValueError` whose message names the field (`title` or `author`).\n3. `POST /books` converts the error to status 400 with JSON `{\"error\": <message>}`.\n4. Nothing is stored; the book list is unchanged.\n\n## Routes\n\nThis feature adds no route of its own; it governs `POST /books` provided by FEATURE-AddBook.md.\n\n## Reads\n\nNone.\n\n## Writes\n\nNone. ReadingList Flask web application. |\n| Depends On | None |\n| Provides | project tree, pytest -q |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nReadingList is a single-reader web application. A reader adds a book with a title and author, views books in the order added, and removes books. Empty titles or authors are rejected with a clear error. The application ships automated tests and a POSIX `bin/test.sh`.\n\n## Technology Stack\n\nDerived from `TECHNOLOGY_STACK.md`; that file is the decision of record.\n\n| Technology | Where it applies |\n|------------|------------------|\n| Python | Implementation language; project tree and environment. |\n| Flask | Web framework; application factory `app_server.create_app()`. |\n| SQLite | Persistence through the generated `persistence` package. |\n| pytest | Automated tests, run as `pytest -q`. |\n| HTML/CSS | Server-rendered pages with minimal CSS, no client-side JavaScript. |\n\n## Project Layout\n\n| Path | Role |\n|------|------|\n| `pyproject.toml`, `uv.lock` | Dependency manifest (Flask, pytest) and committed lockfile. |\n| `.venv` | Environment created by `uv venv`. |\n| `app_server/__init__.py` | `create_app() -> Flask`. |\n| `app_server/testing.py` | `web_client()` plain callable. |\n| `app_server/routes/` | Flask blueprints: JSON routes (`books.py`) and page routes (`pages.py`). |\n| `app_server/services/books.py` | Book service: `add_book`, `list_books`, `remove_book`, `validate_book_input`. |\n| `app_server/templates/` | Jinja templates (base layout, reading list page). |\n| `persistence/` | Generated by Drydock from `DATABASE.md`; never hand-written. |\n| `tests/` | pytest suite with `conftest.py` exposing the `web_client` and `db_session` fixtures. |\n| `bin/test.sh` | POSIX test runner. |\n\n## Module Ownership\n\n| Boundary | Owning module | Allowed low-level access |\n|----------|---------------|--------------------------|\n| Persistence | `persistence` (generated) | The only module that touches SQLite. |\n| Book rules and orchestration | `app_server.services.books` | Calls `db.books.<verb>(...)` only. |\n| HTTP routes | `app_server.routes` | Call the service; never the database. |\n| Configuration | `app_server.__init__` | Resolves `DATABASE_URL`, then `DATABASE_PATH`, then `data/app.db`. |\n| File stores / external services | None | Not used. |\n\n## Layering\n\nRoutes call services; services call `db.<table>.<verb>(...)`. SQL, sessions, table names, and column names never cross the persistence boundary. Books are always returned ordered by id ascending, which is the order added.\n\n## Technical Decisions\n\n- Test runner command is `pytest -q` from the application root.\n- Rejected input returns 400; A valid submission is stored and returned; the new book appears in later list reads.\n\n## Trigger\n\nA client sends `POST /books` with a JSON body.\n\n## Sequence\n\n1. The route parses the JSON body for `title` and `author`.\n2. The service `app_server.services.books.add_book(db, title, author)` stores the book via `books.create`.\n3. The route returns 201 with the stored book.\n\n## Routes\n\n| Route | Behavior |\n|-------|----------|\n| `POST /books` | JSON `{\"title\": ..., \"author\": ...}`. Returns 201 with JSON `{\"id\", \"title\", \"author\"}`. Validation of empty fields is owned by FEATURE-RejectEmptyFields.md. |\n\n## Reads\n\n`books.get` is used to return the stored row.\n\n## Writes\n\n`books.create` inserts one row per valid submission; ids ascend in insertion order.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-BOOK-001` — write: create a book - supplies: `title`, `author` - returns: `id`, `title`, `author`\n- `FEATURE-BOOK-002` — read: retrieve a book by id - supplies: `id` - returns: `id`, `title`, `author`\n\n## Tests\n\nThe build writes pytest tests under `tests/` covering a valid add, the 201 response fields, and a read-back of the stored row.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC addbook-create ===\nIntent: POST /books answers 201 and returns the title and author that were supplied, with an id.\nRequires: python-package=flask; it calls the book service.\n- A stored book keeps exactly the title and author supplied.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-RejectEmptyFields.md ===\n# FEATURE: Reject Empty Fields\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261023 V1 |\n| Description | Reject a submission whose title or author is empty with a clear error and store nothing. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-AddBook.md |\n| Provides | validate_book_input |\n| Consumes | POST /books |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nGuarantee the application never accepts an empty title or empty author. Rejection reports a clear, human-readable error naming the offending field.\n\n## Trigger\n\nA submission to `POST /books` (or the page form route) carries a missing, empty, or whitespace-only title or author.\n\n## Sequence\n\n1. The service calls `validate_book_input(title, author)` in `app_server.services.books` before any store call.\n2. A value that is missing, not a string, or empty after stripping surrounding whitespace raises `ValueError` whose message names the field (`title` or `author`).\n3. `POST /books` converts the error to status 400 with JSON `{\"error\": <message>}`.\n4. Nothing is stored; the book list is unchanged.\n\n## Routes\n\nThis feature adds no route of its own; it governs `POST /books` provided by FEATURE-AddBook.md.\n\n## Reads\n\nNone.\n\n## Writes\n\nNone. A rejected submission writes nothing.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is owned by FEATURE-AddBook.md.\n\n## Tests\n\nThe build writes pytest tests for empty title, empty author, both empty, missing keys, and whitespace-only values, asserting status 400 and that the list is unchanged.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC reject-empty-title ===\nIntent: POST /books with an empty title answers 400 and stores nothing.\nRequires: python-package=flask; scope=test\n\nfrom app_server.testing import web_client\nfrom persistence.testing import committed_session\n\nclient = web_client()\nwith committed_session() as db:\n    before = len(list(db.books.list()))\nresponse = client.post(\"/books\", json={\"title\": \"\", \"author\": \"ac-reject-author\"})\nassert response.status_code == 400\nassert isinstance(response.get_json()[\"error\"], str)\nwith committed_session() as db:\n    after = len(list(db.books.list()))\nassert after == before\n=== END AC reject-empty-title ===\n\n=== AC reject-empty-author ===\nIntent: POST /books with an empty author answers 400 and stores nothing.\nRequires: python-package=flask;
